# Methodology-Wave Instances Registry

> **Provenance**: S88 W9 cleanup (2026-05-06; user observation that
> `.claude/rules/methodology-wave-allowlist.md` had accumulated ~40 rows of
> multi-paragraph rationale prose, structurally identical to a session log).
> Lift-out follows the W9-RULE-CLEANUP precedent (cross-pillar-bridge-anatomy.md
> + epistemic-discipline.md → cross-pillar-bridge-corpus.md + pru-class-corpus.md)
> and discharges `feedback_rules-compensate-missing-structure.md` at the
> rule-file shape (rule + schema + pointer; per-instance prose to registry).

## Cross-link to authoritative rule

The authorizing rule for METHODOLOGY-class classification lives at
`.claude/rules/methodology-wave-allowlist.md` (M4 substrate per
`wave-classification.md §M4`). M4 is satisfied iff the gate-ID appears in
that rule's allowlist table. **This registry is non-authoritative for M4
satisfaction** — gate-IDs only count when they are listed in the rule's
allowlist table. This registry is the per-gate provenance log: it carries
the rationale prose (rule extension landed, registry slot written, K-counter
advance, M1-M4 conjunction enumeration) that was previously co-located in
the allowlist's `rationale` column.

## Schema

Each entry uses the heading form:

```
### {gate_id} ({session}) — {plan-block-SHA}
```

followed by verbatim rationale prose lifted from the previous allowlist
`rationale` column (no edits to content; only re-keyed by gate-ID heading).
The plan-block SHA repeats the value pinned in the allowlist's
`sha256_of_plan_block` column for the same row.

**Three structural notes** (CO-SIGN-WITH-NOTES; no remediation required):

1. **ASCII `P` vs Unicode `Π` projector-symbol downgrade**: The W7c
   emission #3 lexical (corpus row #3 + s89:143 value-field + registry
   §VII.AV.OP-PROJ line 17366) uses ASCII `P_n-s-substrate-distance-1`,
   while the parallel registry entry §VII.AU.OP-PROJ (line 17196 + theorem
   text 17177/17262) uses Unicode `Π^{n_s}_{substrate-distance-1}`.
   In Connes-Moscovici convention, capital Π canonically denotes a
   substrate-side spectral projector lifted under the bridge map; capital
   P canonically denotes an ambient/laboratory-side projector. The
   ASCII downgrade collapses this convention distinction. The parent-rule
   regex `\int.*d.*Tr.*\([ΠP]_[a-z0-9_-]+\)` admits BOTH (the character
   class `[ΠP]`), so the downgrade is regex-admissible — but Connes-
   convention readers downstream may parse the same operator as
   structurally ambient when the substrate-projector reading is intended.
   Forward suggestion: future bridge-corpus rows SHOULD prefer Unicode `Π`
   when the operator is a substrate-side spectral projector image, with a
   parenthetical ASCII alias for keyboard-portability.
2. **Density factor `ρ_BZ(k; τ_fold)` outside positive-match regex**: The
   parent rule's canonical positive-match regex
   `\int.*d.*Tr.*\([ΠP]_[a-z0-9_-]+\)` matches only up to the closing
   parenthesis of the projector argument; the subsequent `· ρ_BZ(k; τ_fold)`
   density-weight is not captured. Structurally, the lab-side OE form
   `Tr_{A_K}(Π^{n_s}_{substrate-distance-1} · ρ_BZ(k; τ_fold))` (per registry
   §VII.AU.OP-PROJ line 17196) IS internally consistent because the
   density factor IS the HKR push-forward of the Peter-Weyl spectral
   measure — but a stronger structural form would write it as
   `Tr(Π · ρ_BZ)` INSIDE a single trace (which the current regex would
   capture) OR include a weighted-form sub-pattern in the parent rule.
   Forward suggestion: corpus §2 audit-pattern documentation could note
   the weighted-vs-bare-projector form distinction explicitly so future
   bridge entries do not silently bifurcate into a "regex matches" vs
   "structural lab observable is more than what the regex captures"
   condition.
3. **§VII.AU.OP-PROJ vs §VII.AV.OP-PROJ registry notation-bifurcation
   for same landing**: The same S89 W7c FWD-C1 landing has TWO co-existing
   registry entries (`§VII.AU.OP-PROJ` at line 17250 with Unicode `Π`
   theorem text; `§VII.AV.OP-PROJ` at line 17335 with ASCII `P` lab-IN
   element). Per the §VII.AV provenance block (line 17337), `§VII.AV` is
   the rerouted slot per the W7c emission chain (line 140 emission #1/#2
   at §VII.AU with element_2_oe_form=False; line 143 emission #3 at §VII.AV
   with element_2_oe_form=True; supersedes chain anchored at
   `c857179040b40224...`). Structurally consistent (the rerouting is
   audit-trail honest) but the dual-entry presence is worth flagging
   because downstream consumers grepping for FWD-C1 bridge-anatomy may
   encounter both. The corpus row #3 cites §VII.AV (rerouted, latest
   non-superseded). Forward suggestion: §VII.AU.OP-PROJ could carry a
   prominent `SUPERSEDED-BY-§VII.AV.OP-PROJ` annotation in its provenance
   block to make the rerouting visible to grep-only readers.

**One-paragraph reasoning summary**: The W7c emission #3 lexical
`∫_BZ d^d k Tr(P_n-s-substrate-distance-1) · ρ_BZ(k; τ_fold)`
correctly captures the substrate-IS / laboratory-IN bridge anatomy at
substrate-distance-1 (Mellin pole `s=3`) when read through the
NCG-axiomatic / spectral-triple lens. The named projector
`P_n-s-substrate-distance-1` is structurally well-defined as the
band-0 spectral-density-of-states projector `P_0(τ_fold)` on the
substrate's Peter-Weyl decomposition at the substrate-distance-1
sector — equivalent to the Unicode `Π^{n_s}_{substrate-distance-1}` of
the §VII.AU.OP-PROJ theorem text; the integration domain
`∫_BZ d^d k · ρ_BZ(k; τ_fold)` is the HKR `L_max → ∞` push-forward
`ι_*` of the substrate's Peter-Weyl spectral measure to the emergent
Brillouin zone (NOT a container); the substrate-distance-1 pole
localization is structurally correct via the τ_fold-pinned band-0
projector and the W7a Sage-QQ exact rational identity
`n_s_FW² − 1 ≡ α_s_canonical` in Q; and the OE-form structural intent
(integration domain + trace + named projector) is satisfied beyond the
mere regex pass. The three structural notes (ASCII vs Unicode
projector-symbol convention, density-factor placement vs regex
coverage, dual §VII.AU/§VII.AV registry-entry notation-bifurcation)
are corpus-clarification opportunities not structural deficiencies; the
primary W1-13 verdict PASS is preserved INTACT and the corpus row #3
